| Minori | Kohane, are you free this weekend? | talk |
| Kohane | This weekend? Um, I am meeting with someone from another school on Saturday, but I'm free on Sunday. | talk |

| Kohane | Really?! | talk |
| Minori | My mom received a pair of tickets from a friend, you see!<br>So she asked me to go enjoy myself with another friend. | talk |
| Minori | Oh, I'm sorry, maybe you don't like theme parks? | talk |

| Kohane | They've added some new attractions recently, but the nostalgic mood hasn't gone away. | talk |

| An | Right back at you, Kohane! | talk |

| Taiga | Hey, this place... | talk |

| Kohane | O-Okay, I don't mind. | talk |
| Taiga | Hey! Anyone home? | talk |
|  | se00104 | Sound |
| Eatery Owner | Well if it isn't Taiga!<br>It's great to see that you're really back! | talk |
| Taiga's Voice | Yeah, I got back a little while ago. | talk |
| Taiga's Voice | Still, I can't believe you upped and moved to a better location. Your old place was gone, so I thought you'd gone out of business or something. | talk |
| Eatery Owner | The live house next to my place also ended up moving, so I decided to do the same. | talk |
| Eatery Owner | But fear not.<br>My storefront may be new and shiny, but what I make is as good as ever. | talk |
| Taiga's Voice | Yeah, I get what you mean. Things might look different on the outside, but nothing's changed on the inside. | talk |
| Kohane | Nothing's changed on the inside...? | talk |
| Kohane | (Oh, come to think of it...) | talk |
| Kohane | (Taiga's been examining all the places we've been visiting in Vivid Street to see whether or not they've changed, and how much if they have.) | talk |
| Kohane | (I guess it would be interesting to see how things change after being away for so long...) | talk |
| Kohane | Hmm... | talk |
| Kohane | (Which leads me to believe that there's a hidden message behind everything we've been doing.) | talk |
| Taiga | Sorry about that.<br>Thanks for letting me take that detour. | talk |
| Kohane | ... | talk |
| Taiga | Is something wrong? | talk |
| Kohane | Huh?! I-I'm sorry...<br>I was just thinking about what you two were talking about... | talk |
| Taiga | Hm? What do you mean? | talk |
| Kohane | The meaning behind what I've been doing with you. | talk |
| Kohane | For example, I've been noticing how you've been taking an interest in how things have changed around here over the years... | talk |
| Taiga | I see. Is that your interpretation...? | talk |
| Kohane | Yes. But I apologize if you think I'm getting the wrong idea. | talk |
| Taiga | Haha. I'd say not quite, but you're on the right track. | talk |
| Kohane | I am? | talk |
| Taiga | Yeah. Want me to tell you a little secret about what we've been doing so far? | talk |
| Kohane | ...!<br>Yes, please! | talk |
| Taiga | Ever hear this before? They say that about 80% of the information we take in comes from what we see. | talk |
| Kohane | Y-Yes, I think I've heard it on TV before... | talk |
| Taiga | You can learn a lot from just people-watching, looking around the city, seeing all the colors and gauging how close or far away certain things are. | talk |
| Taiga | But where does that leave our ears? | talk |
| Kohane | O-Our ears...? | talk |
| Taiga | Yeah.<br>What can we learn from hearing? | talk |
| Kohane | We can... Hear how people feel...? | talk |
| Taiga | Yeah, exactly.<br>That also includes how far away they are and which way they're facing. | talk |
| Taiga | Still, our eyes still end up being more powerful than our ears. Which means there's only so much you can do with sound alone. | talk |
| Taiga | But we're here to sing. | talk |
| Taiga | So the question becomes, “How do you make people see through music?” | talk |
| Kohane | See through music...? | talk |
| Taiga | Yeah.<br>More than that depending on the situation. | talk |
| Taiga | The lyrics for a song don't always describe things that are grounded in reality. There are times when you have to imagine what's going on yourself. | talk |
| Taiga | And it's not just about showing people that. You can also make them remember things from their past... | talk |
| Kohane | R-Right... | talk |
| Taiga | I'm sure you'll understand eventually. | talk |
| Taiga | Anyway, my point is that just singing isn't enough. I want you to at least take that away from this... | talk |
| Kohane | Okay...! | talk |

| Kohane | Sorry about doing this during practice, everyone, but I took some photos for our flyer and really wanted you to take a look at them. | talk |
| Akito | That's all good.<br>It shouldn't take up much time. | talk |
| An | Yeah! I've been looking forward to seeing what kinds of pics you took for us! | talk |
| Kohane | Thanks!<br>So... Here they are in digital form. | talk |
| Akito | Hmm... | talk |
| Akito | Hey, this one with the live house's sign in it looks cool. I like the composition you chose for it too. | talk |
| Toya | Yes, her broad use of negative space adds a nice dimension to this photo. | talk |
| Kohane | Hehe. I really tried with that one. | talk |
| An | Hehe.<br>See? Isn't Kohane amazing? | talk |
| Akito | Why are you the one bragging?<br>Also, quit yammering and start looking through these. | talk |
| Kohane | What do you guys think?<br>Would any of these work as a flyer? | talk |
| An | I like the one that was taken in front of the live house or the one with the back alley in it. | talk |
| An | Oh, the one of Vivid Street's really good too, but so is the one with my place peeking it's head out. | talk |
| An | Everything you took looks way too good, Kohane! I can't decide at all! | talk |
| Toya | I understand how you feel, Shiraishi.<br>Each one has a very nice feel to it. | talk |
| Akito | Saying, “They're all good,” isn't exactly helpful. It's not gonna help us put any flyers together.  | talk |
| An | Yeah, I know... | talk |
| Toya | Azusawa, there's something I'd like to ask you. Would you mind? | talk |
| Kohane | No, what would you like to ask? | talk |
| Toya | Each of your photos feature a building as their subject, but they all seem to be taken from a distance. Is there a reason behind that? | talk |
| Kohane | Oh, um... This event's supposed to be about everyone who performs on Vivid Street, right? | talk |
| Kohane | That's why I want our flyer to capture that. | talk |
| Toya | I see... | talk |
| Kohane | Also, I'm worried that these subjects won't tell the whole or real story... Like I won't be able to capture what's really going on, you know? | talk |
| An | The real story...? | talk |
| Kohane | Uhh... If someone saw a photo of me with no background in it, they probably wouldn't get a good idea of who I really am... | talk |
| Kohane | But if I were to take it in my room, they'd have a better idea since they'll see all the PXL merch and photos I took decorating it. | talk |
| Toya | That makes sense. It brings a person's individuality and other traits to the surface. | talk |
| An | That's so cool, Kohane!<br>I didn't realize you put so much thought into these! | talk |
| Kohane | No, it's just... My dad always tells me to figure out what I really want to take since where a photo's taken and in what situation are so important. | talk |
| Kohane | Vivid Street in particular has an incredibly unique feel to it, so I want to make it so people see that. | talk |
| An | Yeah, this place has a certain warmth or smell to it that you can't find anywhere else! | talk |
| Akito | Yeah, I get what you're trying to say. Singing somewhere else just isn't the same as singing here. | talk |
| Akito | What do you call it...? The air...? The atmosphere...? No, that isn't any different from the feel of this place... | talk |
| Toya | Yes... I'm having trouble coming up with the right word as well... | talk |
| Toya | But I believe that your photos capture the very essence of what we're struggling to describe, Azusawa. | talk |
| An | Yeah, Kohane, you really are the best! What if you end up becoming a professional photographer?! | talk |
| Kohane | What?! Th-There's no way I could become a pro. My dad's the one who taught me all this and he just does it for fun. | talk |
| Kohane | Moreover, I think I'm able to take photos like this now because of my practices with Taiga. | talk |
| An | Huh? Uncle Taiga? | talk |
| Kohane | Yeah. For instance, Taiga showed me how there's only one way from Vivid Street into the back alley in this photo. | talk |
| An | Wait, this alley! It's the shortcut we always take to go to Weekend Garage from the live house on the street that's one block down. | talk |
| Akito | Yeah, we've been using it a lot too. | talk |
| Toya | Yes, we have. It's narrow and a bit difficult to navigate, but it's always fun to see all the graffiti that people have drawn on the walls. | talk |
| Kohane | Exactly! Taiga showed me all these spots that give off that unique Vivid Street vibe. | talk |
| An | Now I get it! | talk |
| An | If you put this much thought into these pics, I kinda think we should have a bit more of a serious discussion as to which to choose. | talk |
| Kohane | I'm glad you feel that way, but we still need time to practice... | talk |
| Toya | That's okay.<br>I'm sure we'll still have plenty of time for that. | talk |
| Akito | Yeah. Also, our flyer's gonna play a big role in how we get the word out about our event. | talk |
| An | Definitely. We're going through all this trouble, so we should try to make a flyer that also tries to surpass RAD WEEKEND! | talk |
| Akito | You're raising the bar way too high. | talk |
| An | No, I'm not!<br>Kohane can do it. | talk |
| Toya | Yes, I agree. | talk |
| Kohane | You guys... | talk |
| Kohane | Thanks! I'll do my best to put a good flyer together! | talk |

| KAITO | So, Kohane, what did you feel during your attempt at sampling? | talk |
| Kohane | Huh? Um... | talk |
| Kohane | I started by listening to An's song and wanted to capture that heart-pounding sensation I felt when I heard it... | talk |
| Kohane | But I like it so much that I ended up using almost all of it. | talk |
| Rin | Wait, almost all of it? | talk |
| Kohane | Yes, but that sort of defeats the purpose of this exercise, doesn't it? | talk |
| Kohane | So, I listened to it over and over again, and chose all the parts that I thought were absolutely necessary. That's what I had you listen to just now. | talk |
| KAITO | Haha. It sounds like you had quite a lot of fun with this! | talk |
| KAITO | If that's the case, Kohane, I think you're doing just fine. | talk |
| Kohane | Huh...? I am...? | talk |
| KAITO | Remember our discussion about songwriters being very meticulous creatures? | talk |
| KAITO | So, what do you think it means to be “meticulous”? | talk |
| KAITO | It's simple!<br>You just have to have a clear image of what you want to do in your mind. | talk |
| Rin | That's all? | talk |
| KAITO | Yes, that's all. | talk |
| KAITO | And I definitely got the sense that you firmly know what you want to do, Kohane. | talk |
| Kohane | ...!<br>R-Really?! | talk |
| Kohane | The truth is... What I felt when I met An for the first time, my desire to thank her for inviting me to form a team, the joy I feel just standing next to her... | talk |
| Kohane | She's given me so much.<br>I want to convey just how exciting things have been for me! | talk |
| Rin | Wow, that's great!<br>I can't wait to hear that song! | talk |
| KAITO | Me neither. I'll definitely want to hear it as well. | talk |
| KAITO | However, Kohane, keep in mind that your feelings and the song you want to create lay within you and only you. | talk |
| KAITO | Thus, it's up to you to give it form and bring it into this world. | talk |
| Kohane | Within me only... | talk |
| Kohane | You're right. I made the decision to do this, so I'm going to go through with it! I have to push my worries aside! | talk |
| Kohane | So, KAITO, Rin, I'm going to try my best! And I'll be looking forward to working with you two! | talk |
| Rin | Whoa! Kohane's so fired up! | talk |
| KAITO | Yes, she is. And don't forget to have fun along the way as you take on this new challenge. | talk |

| Kohane | Let's see... Where do I begin...? | talk |
| Kohane | I don't really know where to start.<br>There's just so much that I've thought, felt and experienced until now. | talk |
| Toya | Yes, I understand. | talk |
| Toya | So, why don't you start by telling me about what you felt when you decided to start singing...? | talk |
| Kohane | Oh, okay...!<br>That seems like a good starting point. | talk |
| Kohane | So, I decided to start singing after seeing An perform at Weekend Garage. | talk |
|  | bgm00000 | Sound |
| Kohane | Phew... I think that's about everything. | talk |
|  | bgm00018 | Sound |
| Kohane | It's amazing to see just how much happened after I decided to start singing. | talk |
| Kohane | Learning how to perform live in front of an audience was one thing, but I forgot how my parents were initially worried about my safety... | talk |
| An | Yeah, I forgot about that too. | talk |
| Toya | Didn't we invite them to an event in order to get them to see what street music is really about...? | talk |
| Kohane | Yeah, I wanted them to understand that there was nothing dangerous about what I was doing or where I was going. | talk |
| Kohane | Which is why I was so happy when my dad came to see us perform. Although, hearing him shout my name was a little embarrassing. | talk |
| An | Yeah, and we all introduced ourselves to him afterwards. Remember what happened with Akito when we did? | talk |
| Toya | Akito was so polite that her dad thought he was much older than the rest of us. | talk |
| An | Yeah!<br>That was so funny. | talk |
| Akito | Geez...<br>You guys don't need to remember stuff like that. | talk |
| Kohane | Hehe. Still, I'm really glad that my parents became more comfortable with what I was doing. | talk |
| Toya | Yes, I was also a little worried, but it's good that everything worked out in the end. | talk |
| Toya | That being said, Azusawa, your dad seems like a very kind person. | talk |
| Kohane | Huh? Y-You think so...?<br>Thanks. | talk |
| Kohane | He does show his kindness in a lot of ways. Like teaching me about photography and buying me cakes from the store. | talk |
| Kohane | But he does also worry a lot sometimes... | talk |
| An | And he has every right to worry!<br>I mean, if I had a daughter as cute as you, I wouldn't want anything happening to her! | talk |
| An | It's also proof that the apple doesn't fall far from the tree. You both seem nice and polite on the outside... | talk |
| An | But you don't hold back when push comes to shove...! | talk |
| Kohane | What? | talk |
| Kohane | (I-I'm like him...?) | talk |

| Kohane | Thanks for inviting me for lunch, you two. | talk |

| Kohane | Yeah! The hamburgers over there were bigger than my face...! | talk |
| Honami | Wow... If they're that big, I don't know if I could finish one all by myself... | talk |
| Kohane | The supermarkets over there were also really big. I almost got lost when we all went shopping together. | talk |
| Ichika | Really...?! I've seen them in the movies, but... | talk |
| Honami | Hehe. But it sounds like a bit of an adventure. | talk |
| Ichika | Also, how did that event go?<br>You said it was called SONIC NOTES, right? | talk |
| Kohane | Oh, yeah!<br>It was such an amazing festival! | talk |
| Kohane | People from all around the world came to watch it, and the atmosphere was incredible right from the start... | talk |
| Kohane | And the venue was so big! It seated 80,000, and the stage was huge! | talk |
| Ichika | 80,000...?! | talk |
| Kohane | Musicians from all sorts of genres were there, and they were all amazing, too! | talk |
| Kohane | Each time a new performer came up, right when they started their first song, the crowd's roar would shake the whole venue...! | talk |
| Honami | People would be that excited right at the start? That is pretty incredible... | talk |
| Kohane | A venue of that size, full of people from all over the world all getting so passionate, and we were right there with them... All of us, totally caught up in the music. | talk |
| Kohane | And my heart raced the entire time! | talk |
| Honami | Hehe. That's wonderful.<br>I'm glad that you got to have such a fun time. | talk |
